9935	If the given worker w is not terminating, it enters a loop where it checks the scanState of w. If scanState is non-negative, it breaks the loop. If scanState is negative, it performs a spin if spins are left, or checks if the worker is terminating or if the current thread is interrupted. If none of these conditions are met, it calculates the number of active workers (ac) and checks if the pool is terminating or if the current worker is the last waiter. If so, it attempts to shrink excess spares or uses a timed wait. If the timed wait is interrupted or the pool is still terminating, it returns false. Otherwise, it breaks the loop and returns true.
